# energy per metre of height gained; descending returns nothing\n}\n\n\nclass Surface:\n    \"\"\"A height grid with bilinear interpolation, and one terrain class per grid cell.\n\n    `elevation_cm[j][i]` is the height in centimetres at x = i * cell, y = j * cell.\n    `terrain[j][i]` is the class code of the cell with that vertex as its south-west corner.\n    \"\"\"\n\n    def __init__(self, cell: float, elevation_cm: Sequence[Sequence[int]], terrain: Sequence[str]) -> None:\n        self.cell = float(cell)\n        self.h: List[List[float]] = [[value / 100.0 for value in row] for row in elevation_cm]\n        self.ny, self.nx = len(self.h), len(self.h[0])\n        if self.ny < 2 or self.nx < 2 or any(len(row) != self.nx for row in self.h):\n            raise ValueError(\"the elevation grid must be rectangular, at least 2 by 2\")\n        if len(terrain) != self.ny - 1 or any(len(row) != self.nx - 1 for row in terrain):\n            raise ValueError(\"terrain needs one code per grid cell\")\n        self.codes: List[str] = [str(row) for row in terrain]\n        self.width = (self.nx - 1) * self.cell\n        self.height = (self.ny - 1) * self.cell\n\n    def inside(self, x: float, y: float) -> bool:\n        return 0.0 <= x <= self.width and 0.0 <= y <= self.height\n\n    def locate(self, x: float, y: float) -> Tuple[int, int, float, float]:\n        \"\"\"The cell (i, j) holding the point and the point's fractions inside it.\"\"\"\n        fx, fy = x / self.cell, y / self.cell\n        i = min(self.nx - 2, max(0, int(fx)))\n        j = min(self.ny - 2, max(0, int(fy)))\n        return i, j, fx - i, fy - j\n\n    def sample(self, x: float, y: float) -> Tuple[float, float, float, str]:\n        \"\"\"Height, d(height)/dx, d(height)/dy and terrain code at a point.\"\"\"\n        cell = self.cell\n        fx, fy = x / cell, y / cell\n        i = min(self.nx - 2, max(0, int(fx)))\n        j = min(self.ny - 2, max(0, int(fy)))\n        tx, ty = fx - i, fy - j\n        low, high = self.h[j], self.h[j + 1]\n        h00, h10, h01, h11 = low[i], low[i + 1], high[i], high[i + 1]\n        height = (h00 * (1 - tx) + h10 * tx) * (1 - ty) + (h01 * (1 - tx) + h11 * tx) * ty\n        gx = ((h10 - h00) * (1 - ty) + (h11 - h01) * ty) / cell\n        gy = ((h01 - h00) * (1 - tx) + (h11 - h10) * tx) / cell\n        return height, gx, gy, self.codes[j][i]\n\n    def elevation(self, x: float, y: float) -> float:\n        return self.sample(x, y)[0]\n\n    def slope(self, x: float, y: float) -> float:\n        _, gx, gy, _ = self.sample(x, y)\n        return math.hypot(gx, gy)\n\n    def terrain(self, x: float, y: float) -> str:\n        return TERRAIN_CODES[self.sample(x, y)[3]]\n\n    def cell_max_slope(self, i: int, j: int) -> float:\n        \"\"\"The steepest slope anywhere in cell (i, j). On a bilinear patch that is at a corner.\"\"\"\n        low, high, cell = self.h[j], self.h[j + 1], self.cell\n        h00, h10, h01, h11 = low[i], low[i + 1], high[i], high[i + 1]\n        south, north = (h10 - h00) / cell, (h11 - h01) / cell\n        west, east = (h01 - h00) / cell, (h11 - h10) / cell\n        return max(math.hypot(south, west), math.hypot(south, east),\n                   math.hypot(north, west), math.hypot(north, east))\n\n\ndef drive(surface: Surface, physics: Dict[str, Any], x: float, y: float, heading_deg: float, metres: float,\n          slip_base: Dict[str, float], noise: float = 0.0, battery: Optional[float] = None) -> Dict[str, Any]:\n    \"\"\"One drive command: `metres` of wheel travel at `heading_deg` from (x, y).\n\n    Per step of `segment_m` wheel metres, with the ground sampled where the step starts:\n      grade  = slope along the heading (rise over run), cross = slope to the left of it\n      slip   = clip(slip_base[terrain] + slip_grade_gain[terrain] * max(0, grade) + noise, 0, slip_max)\n      the rover moves segment * (1 - slip) along the heading and\n      segment * side_slip_gain[terrain] * cross to the right (downhill across the heading)\n      energy = segment * drive_cost_per_wheel_m[terrain] + climb_cost_per_m * max(0, height gained)\n    The step is refused, and the drive ends, if it would leave the map (`stop` = \"map_boundary\")\n    or end on ground steeper than `max_tilt` (`stop` = \"tilt\"). With a `battery`, the drive also\n    ends when the battery is spent (`stop` = \"battery\").\n\n    Returns the end position, wheel and ground metres, the energy used and `stop` (None if the\n    whole command ran).\n    \"\"\"\n    radians = math.radians(heading_deg)\n    ux, uy = math.cos(radians), math.sin(radians)\n    segment_full = physics[\"segment_m\"]\n    gain, side, cost = physics[\"slip_grade_gain\"], physics[\"side_slip_gain\"], physics[\"drive_cost_per_wheel_m\"]\n    climb, slip_max, max_tilt = physics[\"climb_cost_per_m\"], physics[\"slip_max\"], physics[\"max_tilt\"]\n    width, height = surface.width, surface.height\n    remaining = float(metres)\n    wheel = ground = energy = 0.0\n    stop = None\n    here, gx, gy, code = surface.sample(x, y)\n    while remaining > 1e-9:\n        segment = min(segment_full, remaining)\n        name = TERRAIN_CODES[code]\n        grade = gx * ux + gy * uy\n        cross = -gx * uy + gy * ux\n        slip = slip_base[name] + gain[name] * (grade if grade > 0 else 0.0) + noise\n        slip = 0.0 if slip < 0 else (slip_max if slip > slip_max else slip)\n        forward = segment * (1.0 - slip)\n        sideways = segment * side[name] * cross\n        next_x = x + ux * forward + uy * sideways\n        next_y = y + uy * forward - ux * sideways\n        if not (0.0 <= next_x <= width and 0.0 <= next_y <= height):\n            stop = \"map_boundary\"\n            break\n        there, next_gx, next_gy, next_code = surface.sample(next_x, next_y)\n        if math.hypot(next_gx, next_gy) > max_tilt:\n            stop = \"tilt\"\n            break\n        spent = segment * cost[name] + climb * (there - here if there > here else 0.0)\n        if battery is not None and spent >= battery - energy:\n            energy = battery\n            wheel += segment\n            ground += math.hypot(next_x - x, next_y - y)\n            x, y = next_x, next_y\n            stop = \"battery\"\n            break\n        energy += spent\n        wheel += segment\n        ground += math.hypot(next_x - x, next_y - y)\n        remaining -= segment\n        x, y, here, gx, gy, code = next_x, next_y, there, next_gx, next_gy, next_code\n    return {\"x\": x, \"y\": y, \"wheel\": wheel, \"ground\": ground, \"energy\": energy, \"stop\": stop}","lineStart":1,"lineEnd":152,"totalLines":152,"truncated":false}},"title":"app/lightlag_surface.py","time":{"start":1794709621777,"end":1794709622506}},"metadata":{"openai":{"itemId":"fc_0669d5b8ca60d2d0016af91874f8a487d19f8e67cf7d49900b"}},"id":"prt_1dd078c0c001XtjGjii8BQga2J","sessionID":"ses_e22f884daffePqQYSc1Y8om42g","messageID":"msg_1dd077d59001iHFxUVjHc1NdHD"}}

yy=jj*5\n+                    if not self.safe_line(x,y,xx,yy,1.1): continue\n+                    midx=(x+xx)/2; midy=(y+yy)/2\n+                    _,gx,gy,terrain=self.s.sample(midx,midy)\n+                    factor={'b':1.045,'r':1.10,'s':1.29,'k':1.21}[terrain] + .75*math.hypot(gx,gy)\n+                    cost=math.hypot(xx-x,yy-y)*factor\n+                    q=jj*self.nx+ii\n+                    self.adj[k].append((q,cost)); self.adj[q].append((k,cost))\n+        print('graph ready',flush=True)\n+    def field(self,key,x,y):\n+        k=self.idx(x,y)\n+        if not self.adj[k]:\n+            options=[(math.hypot(*[a-b for a,b in zip(self.xy(i),(x,y))]),i) for i in range(self.n) if self.adj[i]]\n+            k=min(options)[1]\n+            print('isolated',key,x,y,'node',self.xy(k),flush=True)\n+        dist=[float('inf')]*self.n; dist[k]=0.; h=[(0.,k)]\n+        while h:\n+            d,p=heapq.heappop(h)\n+            if d!=dist[p]: continue\n+            for q,c in self.adj[p]:\n+                nd=d+c\n+                if nd<dist[q]: dist[q]=nd; heapq.heappush(h,(nd,q))\n+        self.fields[key]=dist\n+    def add_targets(self,targets):\n+        for t in targets:\n+            key=t['id']\n+            if key not in self.targets:\n+                self.targets[key]=t\n+                self.field(key,t['x'],t['y'])\n+                print('target',key,t['x'],t['y'],'value',t['value'],'deadline',t['deadline_tick'],flush=True)\n+    def dist(self,key,x,y):\n+        k=self.idx(x,y); d=self.fields[key][k]\n+        if math.isinf(d):\n+            choices=[]\n+            for dj in range(-2,3):\n+                for di in range(-2,3):\n+                    q=k+dj*self.nx+di\n+                    if 0<=q<self.n and self.adj[q] and self.safe_line(x,y,*self.xy(q),.3):\n+                        choices.append((self.fields[key][q]+math.dist((x,y),self.xy(q))*1.2,q))\n+            return min(choices)[0] if choices else 1e6\n+        return d+math.dist((x,y),self.xy(k))*.6\n+    def waypoint(self,key,x,y):\n+        dest=self.base if key=='base' else (self.targets[key]['x'],self.targets[key]['y'])\n+        if math.dist((x,y),dest)<18 and self.safe_line(x,y,*dest,.8): return dest\n+        k=self.idx(x,y); field=self.fields[key]\n+        if not self.adj[k] or math.isinf(field[k]):\n+            choices=[]\n+            for dj in range(-3,4):\n+                for di in range(-3,4):\n+                    q=k+dj*self.nx+di\n+                    if 0<=q<self.n and self.adj[q] and self.safe_line(x,y,*self.xy(q),.5):\n+                        choices.append((field[q]+math.dist((x,y),self.xy(q))*1.15,q))\n+            if not choices: return dest\n+            k=min(choices)[1]\n+        path=[k]\n+        for _ in range(12):\n+            if not self.adj[k] or field[k]<.01: break\n+            q=min(self.adj[k],key=lambda v: v[1]+field[v[0]])[0]\n+            if field[q]>=field[k]-.001: break\n+            path.append(q); k=q\n+        # Use the furthest path vertex reachable without crossing unsafe ground.\n+        best=self.xy(path[0])\n+        for q in path[1:]:\n+            xx,yy=self.xy(q)\n+            if math.dist((x,y),(xx,yy))>25: break\n+            if self.safe_line(x,y,xx,yy,1.2): best=(xx,yy)\n+            else: break\n+        if math.dist((x,y),best)<1.0 and len(path)>1: best=self.xy(path[1])\n+        return best\n+\n+def plan(nav,pos,tick,sampled,oldroute,iterations=14000):\n+    ids=[k for k in nav.targets if k not in sampled]\n+    if not ids: return []\n+    matrix={}\n+    for a in ids:\n+        matrix['start',a]=nav.dist(a,*pos)\n+        matrix[a,'base']=nav.dist('base',nav.targets[a]['x'],nav.targets[a]['y'])\n+        for b in ids:\n+            if a!=b:\n+                matrix[a,b]=nav.dist(b,nav.targets[a]['x'],nav.targets[a]['y'])\n+    startbase=nav.dist('base',*pos)\n+    def score(route):\n+        elapsed=float(tick); prev='start'; reward=0.\n+        for k in route:\n+            elapsed+=matrix[prev,k]/SPEED+OVERHEAD\n+            target=nav.targets[k]; dl=target['deadline_tick']\n+            if dl is None: value=target['value']\n+            elif elapsed<=dl-3: value=target['value']\n+            elif elapsed>=dl+5: value=target['late_value']\n+            else: value=target['late_value']+(target['value']-target['late_value'])*(dl+5-elapsed)/8\n+            reward+=value; prev=k\n+        elapsed+=(matrix[prev,'base'] if route else startbase)/SPEED\n+        # Keep time for return localization and unmodelled terrain.\n+        excess=max(0,elapsed-405)\n+        return reward - excess*.24 - (elapsed-tick)*.008\n+    route=[x for x in oldroute if x in ids]\n+    cur=score(route); cur=ns\n+        if ns>bestscore: best=new[:]; bestscore=ns\n+    print('PLAN',tick,'pos',tuple(round(z,1) for z in pos),'score',round(bestscore,1),'route',best,flush=True)\n+    return best\n+\n+def run():\n+  with connect('http://lightlag:8000/mcp') as tools:\n+    rules=tools.rules(); battery=pkt['battery']; sampled=set(pkt['sampled_targets'])\n+            for event in pkt.get('events',[]):\n+                print('EVENT',pt,event,flush=True)\n+                if event.get('type')=='target_discovered':\n+                    nav.add_targets(tools.map()['targets']);
